Full content:

# Large Module Refactor Protocol

## Box Logic Requirement

Before any implementation, repair, refactor, prompt update, governance update, or bundle creation, the AI must:

- Identify the active box before implementation.
- State owner paths.
- State files allowed to change.
- State files explicitly out of scope.
- Declare cross-box touches.
- Preserve public contracts.
- Validate the active box and any touched external box.


Version: 5.8
Status: Large-module refactor protocol, unified delivery model
Use: Invoke when a relevant Python file is above 500 lines or the user opens a refactor pass.

Module-size law:
- Ideal: <= 400 lines.
- Maximum: <= 500 lines.
- Above 500 lines: split by responsibility.

Mandatory phases:
Task 0 - Audit only:
- inspect actual file;
- line count;
- public API;
- imports;
- side effects;
- owner box;
- current tests/checks;
- risk;
- proposed validation.
Stop and wait for approval.

Task 1 - Roadmap:
- propose helper folder;
- public API preservation plan;
- manifest plan;
- validator plan;
- tests/checks;
- rollback guidance;
- delivery ZIP contents.
Stop and wait for approval unless the user explicitly authorized implementation.

Task 2 - Implementation:
- create or update files in the sandbox;
- preserve project-relative paths;
- run available validation;
- package final files in one ZIP;
- include _bundle_temp\BUNDLE_MANIFEST_<task_slug>.txt;
- provide Windows validation commands.

Normal decomposition:
- Keep the origin file path and public imports stable.
- Extract by whole top-level private functions, whole internal classes, or whole methods.
- Do not slice statement blocks, loop bodies, or partial functions.
- Use sibling helper folder: <origin_stem>_help.
- Use clear helper names, not utils.py, helpers.py, misc.py, common.py, shared.py, temp.py.
- Define explicit __all__ for public exports.
- Avoid circular imports.

Source-preserving facade option:
Use only when semantic extraction is too risky, such as:
- huge public function;
- central CLI/module;
- previous whole-symbol extraction cannot reduce below 500 lines;
- public behavior must remain stable before deeper maintainability work.

Rules for source-preserving facade:
- Keep the original file as a small public facade.
- Store preserved implementation source in private helper shards.
- Use unique private source-part symbols.
- Keep each generated file below 500 lines.
- Do not duplicate exported shard symbols.
- Preserve behavior as a tactical stabilization step.
- Mark this as not a true maintainability refactor.
- Plan later semantic extraction if maintainability is the goal.

Delivery model:
- Do not create a required install script.
- Do not require a separate backup-script folder.
- Do not require the user to run a install script.
- Deliver the final origin file, helper files, manifests, validators, checks, and tests directly in the ZIP.
- The ZIP must extract directly into <PROJECT_ROOT>.
- The manifest goes in _bundle_temp.

Backup guidance:
For risky changes, advise the user to use version control or create a project
checkpoint before extracting the ZIP. Do not make the correction depend on a
custom install script.

Minimum validation:
- py_compile touched Python files;
- import smoke for the origin module when possible;
- helper manifest validation when helpers are created;
- relevant Tab/box validation;
- Tab 1/Tab 2 safety gates when project canon requires them.

Success markers should be reported in the manifest, not as install-script output.

Final rule:
One large-module target per pass. Audit first, roadmap second, implement after
approval, deliver one direct ZIP, wait for validation before freeze.


---

## v5.8 Addendum - Post-Refactor Fragmentation Audit

After large-module splitting or source-preserving facade work, run a read-only
fragmentation audit before treating the refactor as safe.

### Purpose

Large-module splits can leave unresolved names, missing imports, import leaks,
facade binding gaps, or helper modules that only work by accident. The audit must
find these before the user freezes the refactor.

### Read-only Task 0 audit runner

For complex helper-folder splits, design a Task 0 audit runner that performs:

```text
Phase 1: discover helper modules
Phase 2: AST-based name resolution per module
Phase 3: standard import, runtime API, GUI leak, and binding-gap detection
Phase 4: import smoke and targeted headless smoke when safe
Phase 5: Markdown + JSON report generation

The runner must not modify source. It should write reports only to the approved
maintenance/audit output area for that pass.
Minimum findings

The audit should report:

    unresolved names;

    missing standard-library imports;

    runtime API imports missing after split;

    Qt/GUI symbols leaking into non-GUI helpers;

    facade/helper binding gaps;

    symbols present in sibling helpers but not imported;

    import smoke failures;

    helper files that are not safe to import standalone when the architecture
    expects facade-only import.

False-positive guard

Before applying import-smoke results, ask whether helpers are intended to be
standalone importable or facade-only private shards. A source-preserving facade
may intentionally make helper shards private implementation details.
Validation rule

A large-module refactor is not ready for freeze until:

focused refactor tests pass
fragmentation audit has no critical unresolved findings
Tab 1 architecture validation has Errors 0
Tab 2 workflow validation has fail 0
user validates locally

Change log

    v5.8: Added post-refactor fragmentation audit requirement for helper-folder and
    source-preserving facade refactors.

Correct direction

Yes. The existing large_module_refactor_protocol.md enforces the 400/500-line rule mainly when refactoring. We should extend the doctrine so it applies at module creation time too, but only for code modules, especially Python source files.

The startup should not load the whole large-module prompt every day. It should load a compact startup bridge that says: whenever AI creates or refactors code modules, it must keep them under 500 lines and ideally under 400 lines, unless the file is not a code module.
Exact bridge text I would add
Code Module Size Bridge

When creating or refactoring code modules, enforce the KANDA module-size law from the start of the work:

    Ideal code module size: 400 lines or fewer.

    Maximum code module size: 500 lines or fewer.

    If a new or refactored code module would exceed 500 lines, split it by responsibility before delivery.

    Prefer cohesive helper modules with clear ownership instead of large mixed-purpose files.

    Preserve public contracts and imports when splitting existing modules.

This rule applies to code/source modules, especially .py files.

This rule does not apply to plain text, Markdown, documentation, prompt, manifest, JSON, log, report, or other non-code content files. Files such as .txt, .md, .json, and documentation artifacts may be larger when their purpose requires it.

When a task needs the full refactor methodology, route to large_module_refactor_protocol.md; do not load the full prompt during normal startup unless needed.
Where it should live

Best implementation plan:

    Add that bridge to the beginning-of-session startup stack, likely 05_start_of_day_master_stack.md or the generated startup bridge source that feeds it.

    Update large_module_refactor_protocol.md so the title/rule says creation and refactor, not refactor only.

    Update the routing/navigation wording so AI knows this is a lightweight startup rule, while the full protocol remains on-demand.

    Add validation that checks the generated startup artifacts contain the bridge and that the wording excludes .txt, .md, docs, manifests, and other non-code files from the 500-line limit.

The important nuance: this is not “all files must be <=500 lines.” It is code modules must be <=500 lines. Large .md, .txt, documentation, JSON manifests, prompt files, and logs remain allowed when appropriate.

Then run install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_startup_code_module_size_bridge_v1_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "*") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host

    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
    $global:LASTEXITCODE = 1
    return
}

Then validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_startup_code_module_size_bridge_v1_patch"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile validation\test_startup_code_module_size_bridge_v1.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_startup_code_module_size_bridge_v1.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Startup code module size bridge validation failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}
finally {
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host

    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_freeze_validation_evidence_merge_paradigm_v1_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_prompt_workspace") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force

    $WorkReceiveBlocks = Join-Path $WorkDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"

The error is clear: the validator passed, but the merge script correctly refused to put evidence for feature B into the stale freeze hint for feature A. I will repair the workflow so validation evidence is merged into the matching feature hint, not whatever stale latest_freeze_hint.json happens to point to.

I reproduced the problem in the design: the merge helper validates the target feature, but it only knows how to merge into latest_freeze_hint.json. If latest still points to the prior Error Memory footer feature, it blocks. The repair should let validation pass the current patch ZIP sidecar into the merge helper so it can load the matching hint first.

Correct diagnosis: the validator passed, but the evidence merge step failed because latest_freeze_hint.json still pointed to the previous feature:

latest freeze hint is error-memory-powershell-finally-footer-guard-v1
validation evidence is for freeze-validation-evidence-merge-paradigm-v1

So I made a repair patch that enforces the correct paradigm:

validation evidence merge must be keyed to the current patch ZIP sidecar,
not whatever stale latest_freeze_hint.json currently points to.

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_freeze_validation_evidence_merge_by_patch_zip_v2_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_prompt_workspace") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {

    Write-Host ""

    Clear-Host
}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_lesson_block_schema_gate_v1_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_prompt_workspace") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_lesson_block_schema_gate_v1_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-lesson-block-schema-gate-v1"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Lesson Block Schema Gate"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\patch_governance\validator.py validation\test_error_memory_lesson_block_schema_gate_v1.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_lesson_block_schema_gate_v1.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Error Memory lesson block schema gate validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-lesson-block-schema-gate-v1
ERROR_MEMORY_LESSON_BLOCK_SCHEMA_GATE: active
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

Context:

Task:
Review the error/context or current draft below and fill one Error Memory lesson JSON object. Focus on the actual mistake, why it happened, how it was corrected or should be corrected, and the rule that prevents repeating it. Do not invent validation evidence. If the error has not been corrected yet, set status to draft and explain what is missing.

Critical rules:
- Return only the receive-ready block that can be pasted with Paste error formatted from AI or bundled in an AI-created Error Lesson ZIP.
- Use the evidence in this chat only.
- Do not write a patch.
- Do not include explanations outside the JSON block.
- If an install command, validation command, patch ZIP name, validation output, or freeze evidence is present, summarize it in the matching field.
- Use status active only when symptom, root_cause, correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, and prevention_triggers are meaningful.
- Use status draft if the root cause or correction is not yet known.
- operation_phase is metadata from the error/formulary, not a manual GUI choice; preserve it when supplied.
- Keep language factual/blameless. Use wrong_assumption instead of blame.
- Do not include secrets, usernames, emails, IPs, or private paths unless already scrubbed.
- Allowed keys only: correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, install_command_summary, long_term_prevention, notes, operation_phase, prevention_triggers, raw_error_text, regression_check, root_cause, source_patch_zip, status, symptom, validation_command_summary, validation_evidence, wrong_assumption.

Return format - strict copy/paste contract for KANDA Reasoner Error Memory tab:
The first visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN.
The last visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END.
Between the markers, return one valid JSON object only.
Use double quotes for every key and string value. No markdown. No code fences. No comments. No trailing commas.
For multiline text fields, keep each field as one JSON string and escape line breaks as \n.

}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_export_clipboard_complete_json_v2_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

I understand: after v2, the clipboard is still ending up as E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\second_prompt_files. That means the path is likely being copied by a different “Show Project/export folder” handler after the EM export, or the wrong button/wiring is still active. I’ll inspect the actual GUI wiring and patch the exact clipboard source.

I found the stronger fix: the EM export button still calls the second-prompt export writer internally, so second_prompt_files remains in the button’s execution path. I’m making v3 split the responsibilities: the EM button will no longer use the second-prompt writer at all; it will build/copy the complete JSON directly from Error Memory exports.

You were right again. The stronger fix is: the EM export button must not call the second-prompt export writer at all.

I made v3.

What v3 changes:

    The EM button no longer calls write_error_memory_ai_send_files(...).

    The EM button no longer resolves second_prompt_files.

    It uses a new complete-JSON-only writer:

        write_complete_error_memory_ai_clipboard_export(...)

    It writes backup JSON to:

        project_error_memory/exports/latest_error_memory_complete_for_ai.json

    It copies complete JSON to clipboard.

    It also places the full JSON in the Error Editor as a fallback copy source.

    It keeps second-prompt export behavior separate for Show Project to AI.

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

dont guess , dont assume anything , read code carefully and correct it

I will not guess from the symptom this time. I will inspect the actual exporter, GUI handler, store/save API, and the failing v4 validation test before creating another patch.

I found the concrete v4 validation bug. The test uses a temporary project folder, but on Windows the Error Memory path resolver maps that to a drive-root folder like C:\demo_project_show_project_to_AI or E:\demo_project_show_project_to_AI. That folder can persist across test runs, so counting lesson-*.json == 15 is brittle and can fail even when saving worked.

You were right. I read the code and found the real v4 validation mistake.

The v4 exporter logic was mostly okay, but the v4 validation test was wrong for Windows. It created a temp project, but KANDA resolves Error Memory to a drive-root generated folder like:

E:\demo_project_show_project_to_AI\project_error_memory\lessons

That folder can persist between test runs. So the test counted all lesson-*.json files and expected exactly 15, even though stale test files could already be there.

I also corrected another regression: v4 included too much of kanda_reasoner_app and could overwrite the Error Memory schema gate. v5 restores that gate.

I will fix the exact crash first. The traceback points to one invalid PySide6 enum call in error_memory_tab.py: textCursor().Start is not a valid cursor operation; it must use QTextCursor.Start or avoid moving the cursor that way.

Done:

I corrected the exact crash:

AttributeError: 'PySide6.QtGui.QTextCursor' object has no attribute 'Start'

The bad line was:

self.received_preview_edit.moveCursor(self.received_preview_edit.textCursor().Start)

It is now replaced with the safe PySide6 cursor sequence:

cursor = self.received_preview_edit.textCursor()
cursor.movePosition(cursor.MoveOperation.Start)
self.received_preview_edit.setTextCursor(cursor)

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_export_cursor_start_guard_v6_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
    } elseif (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

in EM tab  button Copy error/ draft to AI is copying the prompt , it must copy just the  error in erro editor, update

Done:

I read the actual button handler. The bug was real:

Copy error/draft to AI

was calling:

build_error_lesson_ai_form_prompt(...)

So it copied a full prompt wrapper instead of the raw Error Editor text.

v7 changes that button to copy exactly:

self.received_preview_edit.toPlainText().strip()

No prompt. No wrapper. No KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN instruction. No operation_phase. Just the Error Editor text.

Box Logic classification:

Box: User-facing PowerShell command shape / ZIP staging
Not: v7 code
Not: validator
Not: GUI handler

The ZIP exists in the canonical root:

E:\kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_editor_only_v7_patch.zip

The failure happened because PowerShell treated elseif and else as separate commands. In an interactive terminal, elseif must be part of the same parsed if { } elseif { } else { } block. Since it was pasted separately, PowerShell rejected it.

Use this paste-safe version. It has no elseif and no else:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_editor_only_v7_patch"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
$ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
$DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
$WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
}

$Staged = $false

if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
    Write-Host "STAGING OK: ZIP already exists in canonical work folder."
    $Staged = $true
}

if ((-not $Staged) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
    Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
    Write-Host "STAGING OK: ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
    $Staged = $true
}

if (-not $Staged) {
    throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
}

Write-Host "Root ZIP: $RootPatchZip"
Write-Host "Root ZIP exists: $(Test-Path $RootPatchZip)"
Write-Host "Work ZIP: $WorkPatchZip"
Write-Host "Work ZIP exists after staging: $(Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)"

Expected result:

STAGING OK: ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder.
Work ZIP exists after staging: True

You are a specialist in KANDA Reasoner, repeat-error prevention, validation engines, safe AI-assisted programming workflows, and project-specific Error Memory governance.

Context:
I have an error, validation failure, patch-install failure, or AI implementation mistake that should become a KANDA Error Memory lesson. The lesson will be stored under the selected project's *_show_project_to_AI/project_error_memory folder and later exported to AI as prevention guidance.

Task:
Review the error/context or current draft below and fill one Error Memory lesson JSON object. Focus on the actual mistake, why it happened, how it was corrected or should be corrected, and the rule that prevents repeating it. Do not invent validation evidence. If the error has not been corrected yet, set status to draft and explain what is missing.

Critical rules:
- Return only the receive-ready block that can be pasted with Paste error formatted from AI or bundled in an AI-created Error Lesson ZIP.
- Use the evidence in this chat only.
- Do not write a patch.
- Do not include explanations outside the JSON block.
- If an install command, validation command, patch ZIP name, validation output, or freeze evidence is present, summarize it in the matching field.
- Use status active only when symptom, root_cause, correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, and prevention_triggers are meaningful.
- Use status draft if the root cause or correction is not yet known.
- operation_phase is metadata from the error/formulary, not a manual GUI choice; preserve it when supplied.
- Keep language factual/blameless. Use wrong_assumption instead of blame.
- Do not include secrets, usernames, emails, IPs, or private paths unless already scrubbed.
- Allowed keys only: correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, install_command_summary, long_term_prevention, notes, operation_phase, prevention_triggers, raw_error_text, regression_check, root_cause, source_patch_zip, status, symptom, validation_command_summary, validation_evidence, wrong_assumption.

Return format - strict copy/paste contract for KANDA Reasoner Error Memory tab:
The first visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN.
The last visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END.
Between the markers, return one valid JSON object only.
Use double quotes for every key and string value. No markdown. No code fences. No comments. No trailing commas.
For multiline text fields, keep each field as one JSON string and escape line breaks as \n.

Copy/paste-ready answer shape:
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
  "status": "active",
  "raw_error_text": "short scrubbed error/context snapshot",
  "operation_phase": "validation",
  "symptom": "what failed",
  "root_cause": "why it failed",
  "wrong_assumption": "the assumption that caused the mistake",
  "correct_fix": "how it was corrected or should be corrected",
  "long_term_prevention": "future workflow/test/architecture rule that prevents recurrence",
  "do_not_repeat_rule": "single clear prevention rule",
  "prevention_triggers": ["trigger phrase 1", "trigger phrase 2"],
  "validation_evidence": ["only real validation evidence present in this chat"],
  "regression_check": {"type": "validation_command", "command": "", "expected_marker": "", "required_before_freeze": false},
  "source_patch_zip": "",
  "install_command_summary": "",
  "validation_command_summary": "",
  "notes": ""
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

What v8 changes:

    Button label becomes: Copy Last Error/Draft to AI

    It no longer copies the full Error Editor blindly.

    It extracts only the latest payload:

        Last KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN...END block, if present.

        Otherwise raw_error_text from the last Current error/draft JSON... section.

        Otherwise plain Error Editor text.

    It blocks prompt-wrapper leakage like You are a specialist..., Task:, Critical rules, and Return format.

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_latest_payload_v8_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
    }

    if ((-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_latest_payload_v8_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-copy-error-draft-latest-editor-payload-v8"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Copy Error Draft Latest Editor Payload v8"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        if (Test-Path $RootPatchZip) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
            }
            Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip)) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory\editor_clipboard.py kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory_gui\error_memory_tab.py scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py validation\test_error_memory_copy_error_draft_latest_editor_payload_v8.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_copy_error_draft_latest_editor_payload_v8.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Error Memory copy latest editor payload validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-copy-error-draft-latest-editor-payload-v8
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_DRAFT: latest editor payload only
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_DRAFT_NO_PROMPT_WRAPPER: enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_DRAFT_EXTRACTS_RAW_ERROR_TEXT: enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_EXPORT_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: preserved
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

You are a specialist in KANDA Reasoner, repeat-error prevention, validation engines, safe AI-assisted programming workflows, and project-specific Error Memory governance.

Context:
I have an error, validation failure, patch-install failure, or AI implementation mistake that should become a KANDA Error Memory lesson. The lesson will be stored under the selected project's *_show_project_to_AI/project_error_memory folder and later exported to AI as prevention guidance.

Task:
Review the error/context or current draft below and fill one Error Memory lesson JSON object. Focus on the actual mistake, why it happened, how it was corrected or should be corrected, and the rule that prevents repeating it. Do not invent validation evidence. If the error has not been corrected yet, set status to draft and explain what is missing.

Critical rules:
- Return only the receive-ready block that can be pasted with Paste error formatted from AI or bundled in an AI-created Error Lesson ZIP.
- Use the evidence in this chat only.
- Do not write a patch.
- Do not include explanations outside the JSON block.
- If an install command, validation command, patch ZIP name, validation output, or freeze evidence is present, summarize it in the matching field.
- Use status active only when symptom, root_cause, correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, and prevention_triggers are meaningful.
- Use status draft if the root cause or correction is not yet known.
- operation_phase is metadata from the error/formulary, not a manual GUI choice; preserve it when supplied.
- Keep language factual/blameless. Use wrong_assumption instead of blame.
- Do not include secrets, usernames, emails, IPs, or private paths unless already scrubbed.
- Allowed keys only: correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, install_command_summary, long_term_prevention, notes, operation_phase, prevention_triggers, raw_error_text, regression_check, root_cause, source_patch_zip, status, symptom, validation_command_summary, validation_evidence, wrong_assumption.

Return format - strict copy/paste contract for KANDA Reasoner Error Memory tab:
The first visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN.
The last visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END.
Between the markers, return one valid JSON object only.
Use double quotes for every key and string value. No markdown. No code fences. No comments. No trailing commas.
For multiline text fields, keep each field as one JSON string and escape line breaks as \n.

Copy/paste-ready answer shape:
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
  "status": "active",
  "raw_error_text": "short scrubbed error/context snapshot",
  "operation_phase": "validation",
  "symptom": "what failed",
  "root_cause": "why it failed",
  "wrong_assumption": "the assumption that caused the mistake",
  "correct_fix": "how it was corrected or should be corrected",
  "long_term_prevention": "future workflow/test/architecture rule that prevents recurrence",
  "do_not_repeat_rule": "single clear prevention rule",
  "prevention_triggers": ["trigger phrase 1", "trigger phrase 2"],
  "validation_evidence": ["only real validation evidence present in this chat"],
  "regression_check": {"type": "validation_command", "command": "", "expected_marker": "", "required_before_freeze": false},
  "source_patch_zip": "",
  "install_command_summary": "",
  "validation_command_summary": "",
  "notes": ""
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

Box Logic classification first: this is the Error Memory GUI clipboard-contract box, specifically the Copy error/draft to AI handler and any helper it calls. I will audit the actual code path before writing a repair, and I will not treat the pasted prompt text as proof of the final root cause until the handler/helper source confirms it.

Audit result so far: the original installed/source code still has the old handler _copy_formulary_prompt_to_ai, and that handler explicitly calls build_error_lesson_ai_form_prompt(...). That explains the prompt-wrapper clipboard output. The v8 patch added a helper, but I’m checking whether the helper’s extraction contract exactly matches “last error code from Error Editor,” not merely “some embedded payload.”

I found the actual v8 mistake by auditing the helper priority order. The old prompt text contains a sample KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN...END answer shape before the real Current error/draft JSON... section. v8 checks receive-ready blocks first, so it can copy the sample block instead of the real last error payload. That is the code-level bug.

You were correct. I audited the code instead of guessing.

Box Logic

Box: Error Memory GUI clipboard contract
Owner files:
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory_gui/error_memory_tab.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/editor_clipboard.py

Not the box:
- ZIP staging
- validation engine
- freeze logic
- Error Memory storage schema

Actual audited bug

v8 was still not safe enough. The old AI prompt contains this sample block before the real error payload:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
...
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

v8 checked KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN...END first. So it could copy the sample answer block from the prompt instructions instead of the real last payload after:

Current error/draft JSON for completion or correction:

v9 fixes priority: the last Current error/draft JSON... payload wins first. Only if that marker is absent will it copy the last receive-ready KANDA block.

catch {

You were right: v9 did not reach code validation. The failure is a different box.

Box Logic classification

Box: patch ZIP governance / Error Memory packaged-lesson JSON
Not: GUI handler
Not: clipboard extraction logic
Not: freeze logic

Your log shows the ZIP contract failed because the packaged Error Memory lesson was malformed JSON:

ZIP CONTRACT: FAIL - Error Memory lesson block is not valid JSON ...
Unterminated string starting at: line 63 column 5

The earlier pasted command also had a terminal paste issue, where Clear-Host$PATCH_NAME was interpreted as one command.

I audited the v9 ZIP. The GUI/helper code was the intended correction, but the staged Error Memory lesson inside the ZIP included the exact receive-ready delimiter text inside a JSON string. That caused the validator to stop reading the block early and parse truncated JSON.

I rebuilt it as v10.

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null

}

}

You are a specialist in KANDA Reasoner, repeat-error prevention, validation engines, safe AI-assisted programming workflows, and project-specific Error Memory governance.

Context:
I have an error, validation failure, patch-install failure, or AI implementation mistake that should become a KANDA Error Memory lesson. The lesson will be stored under the selected project's *_show_project_to_AI/project_error_memory folder and later exported to AI as prevention guidance.

Task:
Review the error/context or current draft below and fill one Error Memory lesson JSON object. Focus on the actual mistake, why it happened, how it was corrected or should be corrected, and the rule that prevents repeating it. Do not invent validation evidence. If the error has not been corrected yet, set status to draft and explain what is missing.

Critical rules:
- Return only the receive-ready block that can be pasted with Paste error formatted from AI or bundled in an AI-created Error Lesson ZIP.
- Use the evidence in this chat only.
- Do not write a patch.
- Do not include explanations outside the JSON block.
- If an install command, validation command, patch ZIP name, validation output, or freeze evidence is present, summarize it in the matching field.
- Use status active only when symptom, root_cause, correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, and prevention_triggers are meaningful.
- Use status draft if the root cause or correction is not yet known.
- operation_phase is metadata from the error/formulary, not a manual GUI choice; preserve it when supplied.
- Keep language factual/blameless. Use wrong_assumption instead of blame.
- Do not include secrets, usernames, emails, IPs, or private paths unless already scrubbed.
- Allowed keys only: correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, install_command_summary, long_term_prevention, notes, operation_phase, prevention_triggers, raw_error_text, regression_check, root_cause, source_patch_zip, status, symptom, validation_command_summary, validation_evidence, wrong_assumption.

Return format - strict copy/paste contract for KANDA Reasoner Error Memory tab:
The first visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN.
The last visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END.
Between the markers, return one valid JSON object only.
Use double quotes for every key and string value. No markdown. No code fences. No comments. No trailing commas.
For multiline text fields, keep each field as one JSON string and escape line breaks as \n.

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
  "status": "active",
  "raw_error_text": "short scrubbed error/context snapshot",
  "operation_phase": "validation",
  "symptom": "what failed",
  "root_cause": "why it failed",
  "wrong_assumption": "the assumption that caused the mistake",
  "correct_fix": "how it was corrected or should be corrected",
  "long_term_prevention": "future workflow/test/architecture rule that prevents recurrence",
  "do_not_repeat_rule": "single clear prevention rule",
  "prevention_triggers": ["trigger phrase 1", "trigger phrase 2"],
  "validation_evidence": ["only real validation evidence present in this chat"],
  "regression_check": {"type": "validation_command", "command": "", "expected_marker": "", "required_before_freeze": false},
  "source_patch_zip": "",
  "install_command_summary": "",
  "validation_command_summary": "",
  "notes": ""
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_editor_exact_json_v11_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-copy-error-editor-exact-json-v11"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Copy Error Editor Exact JSON v11"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory_gui\error_memory_tab.py scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py validation\test_error_memory_copy_error_editor_exact_json_v11.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_copy_error_editor_exact_json_v11.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Error Memory copy exact editor JSON validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-copy-error-editor-exact-json-v11
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_BUTTON: exact Error Editor text copied
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_BUTTON_NO_PROMPT_BUILDER: enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_BUTTON_NO_EXTRACTION_HELPER: enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_EXPORT_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: preserved
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

Expected markers:

ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-copy-error-editor-exact-json-v11
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_BUTTON: exact Error Editor text copied
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_BUTTON_NO_PROMPT_BUILDER: enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_COPY_BUTTON_NO_EXTRACTION_HELPER: enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_EXPORT_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: preserved
FREEZE_HINT_EVIDENCE_MERGE_OK: error-memory-copy-error-editor-exact-json-v11
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_editor_exact_json_v12_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

Context:
I have an error, validation failure, patch-install failure, or AI implementation mistake that should become a KANDA Error Memory lesson. The lesson will be stored under the selected project's *_show_project_to_AI/project_error_memory folder and later exported to AI as prevention guidance.

Task:
Review the error/context or current draft below and fill one Error Memory lesson JSON object. Focus on the actual mistake, why it happened, how it was corrected or should be corrected, and the rule that prevents repeating it. Do not invent validation evidence. If the error has not been corrected yet, set status to draft and explain what is missing.

Critical rules:
- Return only the receive-ready block that can be pasted with Paste error formatted from AI or bundled in an AI-created Error Lesson ZIP.
- Use the evidence in this chat only.
- Do not write a patch.
- Do not include explanations outside the JSON block.
- If an install command, validation command, patch ZIP name, validation output, or freeze evidence is present, summarize it in the matching field.
- Use status active only when symptom, root_cause, correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, and prevention_triggers are meaningful.
- Use status draft if the root cause or correction is not yet known.
- operation_phase is metadata from the error/formulary, not a manual GUI choice; preserve it when supplied.
- Keep language factual/blameless. Use wrong_assumption instead of blame.
- Do not include secrets, usernames, emails, IPs, or private paths unless already scrubbed.
- Allowed keys only: correct_fix, do_not_repeat_rule, install_command_summary, long_term_prevention, notes, operation_phase, prevention_triggers, raw_error_text, regression_check, root_cause, source_patch_zip, status, symptom, validation_command_summary, validation_evidence, wrong_assumption.

Return format - strict copy/paste contract for KANDA Reasoner Error Memory tab:
The first visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN.
The last visible characters must be KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END.
Between the markers, return one valid JSON object only.
Use double quotes for every key and string value. No markdown. No code fences. No comments. No trailing commas.
For multiline text fields, keep each field as one JSON string and escape line breaks as \n.

}

Save the ZIP as:

E:\kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_button_recreated_v13_patch.zip

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_button_recreated_v13_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
$ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
$DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
$WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
$RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
}

$ZipReady = $false

if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
    Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
    $ZipReady = $true
}

if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
    Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
    }
    Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
    $ZipReady = $true
}

if (-not $ZipReady) {
    throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
}

if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
    Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
}

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

$PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
    throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
}

Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

$ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
$ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
$PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
}

Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force

Write-Host "INSTALL OK"

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_copy_error_draft_button_recreated_v13_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-copy-error-draft-button-recreated-v13"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Copy Error Draft Button Recreated v13"

$ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf

$WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

Set-Location $ProjectPath

if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {

}

$ZipReady = $false

in  Error memory tab tell me in plain english  what mark draft  mark active  deprecate  and supersed buttons 

In the Error Memory tab, these buttons control the lifecycle/status of a lesson:
Mark draft

Use this when the lesson is not ready to guide future AI behavior yet.

Plain English:
“This lesson is still incomplete, uncertain, or waiting for validation.”

Use it when:

    root cause is not fully known

    correction is not proven yet

    validation failed or has not run

    the lesson is just a temporary note

A draft lesson should not be trusted as prevention guidance yet.
Mark active

Use this when the lesson is confirmed and ready to prevent repeat errors.

Plain English:
“This is a valid lesson. AI should read it and use it to avoid repeating this mistake.”

Use it when the lesson has:

    clear symptom

    clear root cause

    clear correct fix

    clear do-not-repeat rule

    meaningful prevention triggers

    real validation evidence, when applicable

Active lessons are the main Error Memory lessons that should guide future work.
Deprecate

Use this when the lesson is old, no longer recommended, or replaced by better logic, but you still want to keep it for history.

Plain English:
“This lesson used to matter, but do not rely on it anymore.”

Use it when:

    the architecture changed

    the workflow changed

    the lesson is outdated

    the lesson is too weak or incomplete

    a better lesson exists, but you do not need a direct replacement link

Deprecated lessons should usually not guide future AI behavior, except as historical context.
Supersede

Use this when this lesson is replaced by another specific better lesson.

Plain English:
“This lesson is replaced by a newer/better lesson. Use the newer one instead.”

Use it when:

    v1 was wrong or incomplete

    v2 corrected the root cause

    a more precise lesson now exists

    two lessons overlap, and one should clearly win

Example:

Old lesson: patch ZIP staging issue v1
New lesson: canonical root/work folder staging gate v2

Then the old lesson should be superseded by the newer one.

Simple rule:

Draft = not ready yet
Active = use this lesson
Deprecated = old, do not rely on it
Superseded = replaced by a specific better lesson

Decision: no new app patch is needed. The v14 install can remain installed. The regression was in the install command I gave, because it ended with:

Write-Host "INSTALL OK"

when it should have ended with the 5-second clean terminal behavior.

Use this footer for successful installs:

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

And the install command should keep this structure:

$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    # install work here
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Never deliver a KANDA install block without the 5-second success clear footer.

Yes — in cases like this, heuristic correction is possible and better than requiring AI every time.

But only for mechanical / structural fixes, not for semantic judgment.
Box Logic

Box: Error Memory intake normalization
Goal: clean safe, obvious formatting/schema issues before Memorize Error
Not the box: AI reasoning, root-cause invention, validation evidence invention

For this pasted lesson, the app could safely fix several things heuristically because the information is already present:

Can be heuristic:
- remove KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END wrapper
- parse JSON
- add schema_version if missing
- add project_slug from selected project
- preserve status
- preserve operation_phase
- normalize regression_check
- keep existing validation_evidence
- reject or warn if required active fields are empty

AI is not always needed.
When heuristic correction is safe

Use heuristic correction when the app is only doing obvious cleanup:

Missing schema_version -> add "1.0"
Missing project_slug -> add selected project slug
Receive-ready wrapper present -> remove wrapper before editing/memorizing
Valid JSON but field order messy -> reorder fields
Empty optional fields -> keep empty or remove depending schema
Backslash escaping in command strings -> preserve valid JSON escaping

This is deterministic. The app does not need AI for that.
When AI correction is still needed

AI is needed when the missing part requires understanding:

root_cause is empty
correct_fix is empty
do_not_repeat_rule is weak or missing
symptom is vague
validation_evidence is missing or mixed with guessed evidence
raw_error_text contains a huge log and needs summarization
two different errors are mixed in the same editor
status active is unsafe because correction was not proven

In those cases, heuristic correction should not invent the lesson.
Best rule for KANDA

I would use a 3-level system:

Level 1 — Auto-normalize:
Safe formatting/schema fixes. No AI needed.

Level 2 — Mark draft + warning:
JSON is readable, but required meaning is incomplete.

Level 3 — AI-assisted correction:
Root cause, fix, prevention rule, or evidence needs interpretation.

For your exact example, the app could probably auto-normalize it because it already has:

status
symptom
root_cause
correct_fix
do_not_repeat_rule
prevention_triggers
validation_evidence
regression_check
source_patch_zip

The only likely missing structural fields are things like:

schema_version
project_slug

So yes: the app can heuristically correct this kind of lesson before memorizing, as long as it only adds/normalizes mechanical fields and never invents semantic content.

The prevention rule should be:

Heuristic correction may repair structure, wrappers, schema defaults, and formatting, but must never invent root cause, correction, prevention rules, or validation evidence.
