- validation_evidence

Save the ZIP as:

E:\kanda_error_memory_heuristic_correction_button_v16_patch.zip

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_heuristic_correction_button_v16_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_heuristic_correction_button_v16_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-heuristic-correction-button-v16"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Heuristic Correction Button v16"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory\heuristic_normalizer.py kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory_gui\error_memory_tab.py scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py validation\test_error_memory_heuristic_correction_button_v16.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_heuristic_correction_button_v16.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Error Memory heuristic correction button validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-heuristic-correction-button-v16
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: green unlocked Level 1 enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: red locked Level 2/3 enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: updates Error Editor and AI-assisted intake
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: no semantic invention enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: copy button and pending-intake behavior preserved
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

Expected markers:

ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-heuristic-correction-button-v16
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: green unlocked Level 1 enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: red locked Level 2/3 enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: updates Error Editor and AI-assisted intake
ERROR_MEMORY_HEURISTIC_BUTTON: no semantic invention enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: copy button and pending-intake behavior preserved
FREEZE_HINT_EVIDENCE_MERGE_OK: error-memory-heuristic-correction-button-v16
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE

After validation, fully close and reopen the app before testing the new button.

correct

when a json is updated in AI assisted error  lesson intake  after button "Memorize Error" is pushed  also update with same error json  the window Error Editor

Box Logic classification: this is the AI-assisted intake ↔ Error Editor synchronization box after Memorize Error. I’ll patch only the post-save auto-load flow so whenever the next pending JSON appears in AI-assisted intake, the same JSON is mirrored into Error Editor.

Audit point: v16 already tries to load the next pending lesson after save, but the sync is not explicit as a post-Memorize Error invariant. I’m going to make that invariant named and validated: when the post-save loader places the next JSON into AI-assisted intake, Error Editor must be updated from the same parsed lesson in the same action.

Implemented as a boxed patch.

Box Logic

Box: Error Memory post-Memorize window synchronization
Owner: kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory_gui/error_memory_tab.py

Goal:
After Memorize Error saves/consumes the current pending lesson, if the next pending JSON is loaded into AI-assisted error lesson intake, the same parsed JSON is also loaded into Error Editor.

Out of scope:
- Copy error/draft button
- Heuristic Correction button semantics
- lesson storage schema
- freeze logic
- ZIP staging logic

Sandbox validation passed:

ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-memorize-next-syncs-editor-v17
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: AI-assisted intake JSON mirrored to Error Editor
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: post-Memorize loader uses same parsed lesson
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: current windows clear before next load preserved
ERROR_MEMORY_BOX_LOGIC: window synchronization only
ERROR_MEMORY_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: copy button, heuristic button, pending consume preserved

Save the ZIP as:

E:\kanda_error_memory_memorize_next_syncs_editor_v17_patch.zip

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_memorize_next_syncs_editor_v17_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_memorize_next_syncs_editor_v17_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-memorize-next-syncs-editor-v17"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Memorize Next Syncs Editor v17"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory_gui\error_memory_tab.py kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory\heuristic_normalizer.py scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py validation\test_error_memory_memorize_next_syncs_editor_v17.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_memorize_next_syncs_editor_v17.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Error Memory post-Memorize editor sync validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-memorize-next-syncs-editor-v17
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: AI-assisted intake JSON mirrored to Error Editor
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: post-Memorize loader uses same parsed lesson
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: current windows clear before next load preserved
ERROR_MEMORY_BOX_LOGIC: window synchronization only
ERROR_MEMORY_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: copy button, heuristic button, pending consume preserved
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

Expected markers:

ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-memorize-next-syncs-editor-v17
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: AI-assisted intake JSON mirrored to Error Editor
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: post-Memorize loader uses same parsed lesson
ERROR_MEMORY_MEMORIZE_NEXT_SYNC: current windows clear before next load preserved
ERROR_MEMORY_BOX_LOGIC: window synchronization only
ERROR_MEMORY_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: copy button, heuristic button, pending consume preserved
FREEZE_HINT_EVIDENCE_MERGE_OK: error-memory-memorize-next-syncs-editor-v17
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE

are they the same ? if not they are not updating together

Box Logic classification: this is the window synchronization/rendering contract box. The two windows are using the same lesson data, but not the same rendered text; I’ll separate “same parsed JSON” from “same visible text” before deciding the right fix.

No — they are not the same visible text.

They are the same lesson content semantically, but not the same rendered text.

Box Logic

Box: Error Memory window synchronization / rendering contract
Current behavior: same parsed lesson data, different visible rendering
Problem: windows are not text-identical

Main differences:

AI-assisted intake:
- Has KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END wrapper
- Field order starts with schema_version, lesson_id, status, project_slug...
- Shows receive-ready format

Error Editor:
- Has only raw JSON object
- No KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END wrapper
- Different field order

So the current v17 behavior is:

PASS: both windows contain the same lesson data
FAIL: both windows do not contain the same exact text

Your conclusion is correct: if the requirement is “they update together with the same error JSON,” then this is not strict enough.

The correct next fix should be:

When the next pending lesson is loaded after Memorize Error:
1. Parse the pending lesson once.
2. Render one canonical JSON string once.
3. Set that exact same canonical JSON string into:
   - AI-assisted error lesson intake
   - Error Editor

I would make both windows show the same raw JSON object without the KANDA wrapper, because the Error Editor is supposed to edit the JSON itself. The receive-ready wrapper should only be used for paste/import/export, not for editor synchronization.

Correct rule:

After Memorize Error loads the next pending lesson, AI-assisted intake and Error Editor must be byte-for-byte identical after trimming whitespace.

if both are comceptualy the same why they are diferent?

They are different because the app is showing the same lesson through two different renderers.

Box Logic

Box: Error Memory window rendering/synchronization
Problem: same parsed lesson data, different text representation
Not: lesson content problem
Not: Memorize Error save problem
Not: AI correction problem

What is happening:

AI-assisted intake window
= original receive-ready lesson text
= includes KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END
= preserves the intake-file style/order

Error Editor window
= parsed JSON object
= wrapper removed
= re-rendered by the app as normal editable JSON
= keys appear in a different order

So yes, they are conceptually the same because they contain the same lesson fields and values. But they are not text-identical because:

1. One has KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END, the other does not.
2. One preserves intake/import format.
3. The other is normalized editor JSON.
4. JSON key order does not matter semantically, but it changes visible text.

Example: this is the same conceptually:

{
  "status": "active",
  "lesson_id": "x"
}

and:

{
  "lesson_id": "x",
  "status": "active"
}

Same JSON meaning, different visible order.

The confusing part is the UI expectation. If the goal is:

Both windows must show the exact same text

then current behavior is wrong.

If the goal is:

Both windows must represent the same lesson

then current behavior is acceptable.

For your workflow, I think the better rule is:

AI-assisted intake may keep the receive-ready wrapper.
Error Editor should show clean editable JSON.
But after loading the next pending item, both must be generated from the same parsed lesson object.

So the difference is not necessarily a bug anymore. It is a rendering/design choice.

One important warning: in both pasted versions, this field must remain escaped as \\ inside valid JSON:

"command": "python validation\\test_error_memory_memorize_clears_intake_only_v2.py"

If the actual editor shows only:

"command": "python validation\test_error_memory_memorize_clears_intake_only_v2.py"

then that text is not valid JSON because \t becomes a tab escape.

which of the versions goes to lessons group to be stored?

The version that should go to the Lessons group is the clean parsed JSON object, not the receive-ready wrapper text.

So conceptually:

Stored in Lessons group:
Error Editor version / normalized JSON object

Not this outer wrapper:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
...
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

Plain English

The AI-assisted error lesson intake window is an import/staging format. It can contain:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{ ... }
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

But when you click Memorize Error, KANDA should:

1. read the intake text
2. remove the wrapper
3. parse the JSON
4. validate/normalize it
5. save the parsed lesson JSON into Lessons

So the stored lesson should be equivalent to the Error Editor JSON, not the wrapper text.
Important detail

The stored JSON does not need to preserve the same field order as the intake text. This is okay:

{
  "lesson_id": "x",
  "status": "active"
}

and this is also the same JSON meaning:

{
  "status": "active",
  "lesson_id": "x"
}

But the stored lesson must preserve all required fields, including things like:

schema_version
project_slug
lesson_id
status
raw_error_text
raw_error_snapshot_scrubbed
symptom
root_cause
correct_fix
do_not_repeat_rule
prevention_triggers
redaction
validation_evidence

Best rule

AI-assisted intake = receive/import format
Error Editor = editable normalized lesson JSON
Lessons group = validated normalized JSON lesson, without KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN/END


when I send json  for AI to correct , is it the same to send  any of the versions

Mostly yes, if both versions contain the same valid JSON lesson, AI can understand either one.

But for your workflow, they are not equally ideal.
Best version to send AI for correction

Use the AI-assisted intake version:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
  ...
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

Why: it tells AI clearly:

This is a KANDA Error Memory lesson.
Return the corrected receive-ready block.

That reduces ambiguity.
Acceptable version

The Error Editor raw JSON is also acceptable, but only if you add instruction like:

Correct this KANDA Error Memory JSON and return it inside KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END.

Otherwise AI may return only plain JSON, or may not preserve the expected paste/import format.
Important difference

AI-assisted intake version = best for AI correction / paste-ready roundtrip
Error Editor version = best for editing/storing inside the app
Lessons group version = normalized stored JSON without wrapper

Practical rule

To send to AI for correction: use AI-assisted intake version.
To store in Lessons: use normalized Error Editor JSON.

One warning: if you send the Error Editor version, make sure command paths keep double backslashes:

"command": "python validation\\test_error_memory_memorize_clears_intake_only_v2.py"

not:

"command": "python validation\test_error_memory_memorize_clears_intake_only_v2.py"

The second can become invalid JSON because \t is interpreted as a tab escape.

so create a button "Copy error/draft" also for Ia lesson intake group , that copies content of intake window only

Box Logic classification: this is the AI-assisted intake clipboard button box. I’ll add a separate Copy error/draft button inside the AI-assisted lesson intake group that copies only that intake window’s visible text, without changing the existing Error Editor copy button.

Implemented as a boxed GUI clipboard change.

Box Logic

Box: Error Memory AI-assisted intake clipboard button
Owner: kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory_gui/error_memory_tab.py

Goal:
Add a separate Copy error/draft button inside the AI-assisted error lesson intake group.

Behavior:
Copies only the visible text from the AI-assisted intake window.

Out of scope:
- Error Editor Copy error/draft button
- Heuristic Correction button
- Memorize Error behavior
- lesson storage
- freeze logic
- ZIP staging logic

What v18 adds:

AI-assisted error lesson intake group:

Save the ZIP as:

E:\kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_copy_error_draft_button_v18_patch.zip

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_copy_error_draft_button_v18_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_copy_error_draft_button_v18_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-ai-intake-copy-error-draft-button-v18"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory AI Intake Copy Error Draft Button v18"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory_gui\error_memory_tab.py scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py validation\test_error_memory_ai_intake_copy_error_draft_button_v18.py

VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace

}

Write-Host ""

Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

I restored the split rendering rule:

AI-assisted error lesson intake window

Error Editor

Sandbox validation passed:

ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-ai-intake-pending-format-v19
ERROR_MEMORY_AI_INTAKE_PENDING_FORMAT: pending file layout preserved in AI-assisted intake
ERROR_MEMORY_AI_INTAKE_PENDING_FORMAT: KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON wrapper preserved

Save the ZIP as:

E:\kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_pending_format_v19_patch.zip

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_pending_format_v19_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_pending_format_v19_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-ai-intake-pending-format-v19"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory AI Intake Pending Format v19"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory_gui\error_memory_tab.py scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py validation\test_error_memory_ai_intake_pending_format_v19.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_ai_intake_pending_format_v19.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "AI-assisted intake pending format validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-ai-intake-pending-format-v19
ERROR_MEMORY_AI_INTAKE_PENDING_FORMAT: pending file layout preserved in AI-assisted intake
ERROR_MEMORY_AI_INTAKE_PENDING_FORMAT: KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON wrapper preserved
ERROR_MEMORY_AI_INTAKE_PENDING_FORMAT: Error Editor remains normalized JSON
ERROR_MEMORY_AI_INTAKE_PENDING_FORMAT: post-Memorize auto-load uses split renderer
ERROR_MEMORY_PREVIOUS_GUARDS: intake copy, editor copy, heuristic, pending consume preserved
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

Here is the end-of-chat handoff. Paste this into the next chat so we keep the full logic line.
KANDA End-of-Chat Handoff — Error Memory AI-Assisted Intake / Error Editor Logic
1. Context: what we are doing

We are working on the KANDA Reasoner project, specifically the Error Memory GUI inside the KANDA app.

Project root:

E:\kanda_reasoner

Dynamic Show Project / Error Memory root:

E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\project_error_memory

The current work is focused on the Error Memory tab, especially the relationship between these two windows/groups:

1. AI-assisted error lesson intake window
2. Error Editor window

The purpose of the Error Memory workflow is:

Patch ZIP -> install -> installer stages Error Memory lesson into pending intake folder -> GUI auto-loads pending lesson into Error Memory tab -> user reviews/corrects -> user clicks Memorize Error -> lesson is saved into active Lessons group.

The key design distinction we established is:

AI-assisted error lesson intake window
= receive-ready / AI roundtrip format
= must show KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END
= best format to copy and send to AI for correction

Error Editor window
= clean editable normalized JSON
= no KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END wrapper
= best format for editing and storing into Lessons group

Lessons group storage
= parsed validated normalized JSON object
= no wrapper

So the two windows may contain the same conceptual lesson, but they must intentionally show different text formats.

The AI-assisted intake window is not supposed to look like the Error Editor when auto-populated. It must show the receive-ready block:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
...
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

The Error Editor must show only raw editable JSON:

{
  ...
}

2. Important implementation logic and intended continuation

The current correct behavior after the latest fix is:

Manual paste into AI-assisted intake:
- preserve receive-ready block
- install/correction works

Automatic pending lesson auto-load:
- AI-assisted intake must show receive-ready block with KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END
- Error Editor must show normalized editable JSON only

The split-rendering rule is now canon:

AI-assisted intake = pending receive-ready text
Error Editor = normalized editable JSON

The next assistant must not try to make both windows byte-identical. That was a temporary idea, but we rejected it because it breaks the intended UX.

Correct final rule:

Both windows can represent the same parsed lesson.
They should not necessarily show the same visible text.
AI-assisted intake keeps wrapper/import format.
Error Editor keeps clean JSON format.

Important JSON correction rule

When correcting a lesson for AI-assisted intake, return:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
  ...
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

But when storing into Lessons, the app should save only the parsed JSON object, without wrapper.

Also preserve escaped Windows paths inside JSON:

"command": "python validation\\test_error_memory_memorize_clears_intake_only_v2.py"

Do not output invalid JSON like:

"command": "python validation\test_error_memory_memorize_clears_intake_only_v2.py"

because \t may become a tab escape.
3. What we did in this chat
A. Clarified the two window formats

We compared:

AI-assisted intake:
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{ ... }
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

versus:

Error Editor:
{ ... }

Conclusion:

They may be conceptually the same lesson, but they are different visible renderings.

We clarified:

To send to AI for correction:
Use the AI-assisted intake version with wrapper.

To store in Lessons:
Use the normalized Error Editor JSON / parsed JSON object.

kanda_error_memory_ai_intake_copy_error_draft_button_v18_patch.zip

Add a separate Copy error/draft button inside the AI-assisted error lesson intake group.

Expected behavior:

AI-assisted intake Copy error/draft:
- copies only the visible text from the AI-assisted intake window
- does not use Error Editor text
- does not parse/reformat/build prompt

Error Editor Copy error/draft:
- remains separate
- copies only Error Editor text

D. Regression found after v18

After v18, the user observed:

When manually passing receive-ready text into AI-assisted intake, it worked correctly.

But when the app automatically populated the AI-assisted intake window from pending logic, it populated using the Error Editor normalized JSON format.

That was wrong.

The AI-assisted intake automatic load should not show:

{
  "correct_fix": "...",
  "created_at_utc": "...",
  ...
}

It should show:

KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN
{
...
}
KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END

6. How to continue in the next chat

Start by recognizing this as a KANDA project-maintenance continuation.

Do not re-litigate the design. The correct design is:

AI-assisted intake = receive-ready wrapper format
Error Editor = normalized editable JSON
Lessons group = parsed normalized JSON storage

Then ask or proceed based on what the user wants:
If user says “freeze this”

Proceed with Freeze Feature After Update for v19.

Use Routed Work Path because freeze is governed project memory.

Freeze only under:

project_freeze_after_update/frozen_features_memory

Do not use:

project_freeze_ledger

Freeze entry should say that v19 protects the split rendering contract:

- AI-assisted intake automatic pending load preserves KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN / END.
- Error Editor remains clean normalized JSON.
- Post-Memorize auto-load uses split renderer.
- Intake copy and editor copy buttons remain separate.

If user says “create another patch”

Apply Box Logic first.

Likely related boxes:

Error Memory AI-assisted intake rendering/layout box
Error Memory clipboard button box
Error Memory pending auto-loader box
Error Memory memorize-consume-next-pending box

Patch only the relevant box. Do not modify unrelated freeze logic, installer staging logic, or router logic unless the user asks.
If user reports a new regression

First classify:

Is the issue:
- intake window rendering?
- Error Editor rendering?
- copy button source?
- Memorize Error consume/auto-load?
- pending file staging?
- freeze hint validation?
- terminal footer?

Then patch only that box.
If creating another patch ZIP

Respect KANDA patch delivery rules:

- ZIP contains only updated files.
- Save/download ZIP to drive root first:
  E:\<patch>.zip

- Installer stages it to:
  E:\kanda_reasoner_delete_after_daily_work\<patch>.zip

- Installer derives drive dynamically from $PROJECT_ROOT.

- Installer stages Error Memory lessons into:
  E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake

Terminal footer rule

Install success must always end with:

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Install errors and validation/diagnostic blocks must use:

Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

Do not use detached/trailing PowerShell finally {} blocks.

AI is sending pratically all jsons with errors , update logic of creation of error  text  that are send do "AI assisted error lesson  intake" to send error text correct for memorization , for example the case of text above ,  but do not guess , do not assume anything , read project logic carefully and updated based on facts

I’ll treat this as a new boxed fix: Error Memory lesson-generation contract, not the intake renderer. I’ll inspect the current v19 source and validation files first, then patch only the logic that creates/stages AI-assisted lesson text so generated lessons are active-ready before they reach the intake window.

I found two factual causes in the current project logic, not just in the JSON example: the startup canon still has an older minimal receive-block template near the top, and error_memory/intake.py still tells AI “allowed keys only” with an outdated key set. That can directly cause AI to omit raw_error_text, raw_error_snapshot_scrubbed, redaction, exception, and fingerprint.

Implemented v20 to fix the creation logic, not just manually correct one JSON block.

I inspected the project logic and found the factual cause: the project still had an older minimal Error Memory lesson template in the startup canon, and error_memory/intake.py still used an outdated “allowed keys” set that excluded fields now required for active-ready memorization, such as raw_error_text, raw_error_snapshot_scrubbed, redaction, exception, and fingerprint.

What v20 changes:

Box: Error Memory active-ready lesson creation contract

Updated:

- error_memory_ai_formulary_startup_canon.meta.json
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/intake.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/models.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/schema.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/patch_governance/validator.py
- validation/test_error_memory_active_ready_creation_contract_v20.py

Preserved:
- v19 split renderer
- AI-assisted intake wrapper layout
- Error Editor normalized JSON layout
- Copy error/draft buttons
- Memorize Error pending consume / auto-load behavior

Save the ZIP as:

E:\kanda_error_memory_active_ready_creation_contract_v20_patch.zip

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_active_ready_creation_contract_v20_patch"
$PAYLOAD_FOLDER = "payload"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$InstallFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $ExtractDir = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + "_extract")

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        Write-Host "ZIP already staged in canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        Write-Host "ZIP copied from drive root to canonical work folder."
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ExtractDir) {
        Remove-Item -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExtractDir -Force | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -Path $WorkPatchZip -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

    $PayloadDir = Join-Path $ExtractDir $PAYLOAD_FOLDER
    if (-not (Test-Path $PayloadDir)) {
        throw "Patch payload folder was not found inside the staged ZIP."
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_reasoner_app") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "kanda_prompt_workspace") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "scripts") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "validation") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $PayloadDir "_bundle_temp") -Destination $ProjectPath -Recurse -Force

    $ReceiveBlocksSource = Join-Path $PayloadDir "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    $ShowProjectRoot = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $PendingIntakeDir = Join-Path $ShowProjectRoot "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (Test-Path $PendingIntakeDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PendingIntakeDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $ReceiveBlocksSource "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt") -Destination $PendingIntakeDir -Force
}
catch {
    $InstallFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

if (-not $InstallFailed) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}

Validate:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_error_memory_active_ready_creation_contract_v20_patch"
$FEATURE_ID = "error-memory-active-ready-creation-contract-v20"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Error Memory Active-Ready Creation Contract v20"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ValidationFailed = $false

try {
    $ProjectPath = (Resolve-Path $PROJECT_ROOT).Path
    $ProjectName = Split-Path $ProjectPath -Leaf
    $DriveRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($ProjectPath)
    $WorkDir = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($ProjectName + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $RootPatchZip = Join-Path $DriveRoot ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WorkPatchZip = Join-Path $WorkDir ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EvidencePath = Join-Path $WorkDir ($FEATURE_ID + "_validation_evidence.txt")

    Set-Location $ProjectPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $WorkDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WorkDir -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $ZipReady = $false

    if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if ((-not $ZipReady) -and (Test-Path $RootPatchZip)) {
        Copy-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Destination $WorkPatchZip -Force
        if (Test-Path $WorkPatchZip) {
            Remove-Item -Path $RootPatchZip -Force
        }
        $ZipReady = $true
    }

    if (-not $ZipReady) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found in canonical locations only: $RootPatchZip or $WorkPatchZip"
    }

    python scripts\validate_patch_zip.py $WorkPatchZip
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "ZIP contract validation failed."
    }

    python -m py_compile `
        kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory\intake.py `
        kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory\models.py `
        kanda_reasoner_app\error_memory\schema.py `
        kanda_reasoner_app\patch_governance\validator.py `
        scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py `
        validation\test_error_memory_active_ready_creation_contract_v20.py

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Python compile validation failed."
    }

    python validation\test_error_memory_active_ready_creation_contract_v20.py
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Active-ready creation contract validation failed."
    }

    $EvidenceText = @"
ZIP CONTRACT: PASS
VALIDATION OK: error-memory-active-ready-creation-contract-v20
ERROR_MEMORY_ACTIVE_READY_CREATION: obsolete minimal AI prompt removed
ERROR_MEMORY_ACTIVE_READY_CREATION: active-ready keys required in AI creation prompt
ERROR_MEMORY_ACTIVE_READY_CREATION: raw_error_text and raw_error_snapshot_scrubbed enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_ACTIVE_READY_CREATION: redaction, exception, fingerprint enforced
ERROR_MEMORY_ACTIVE_READY_CREATION: package validator blocks non-memorizable active lessons
VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE
"@

    Set-Content -Path $EvidencePath -Value $EvidenceText -Encoding UTF8

    python scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py --project-root $ProjectPath --feature-id $FEATURE_ID --feature-title $FEATURE_TITLE --patch-zip $WorkPatchZip --evidence-file $EvidencePath
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Freeze hint validation evidence merge failed."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION COMMAND COMPLETE"
}
catch {
    $ValidationFailed = $true
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION ERROR. Review the error below before clearing the terminal."
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    if ($_.ScriptStackTrace) {
        Write-Host $_.ScriptStackTrace
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Clear-Host
Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
Clear-Host

After install/validation, future AI-created Error Memory receive blocks should be generated from the full active-ready contract. Already-staged broken pending lessons will not be magically fixed; those should be corrected, memorized as draft, or removed from pending intake.
